human-b05c2cca08eda293
Source
Defect report, row 2; scope: global; element: Region
Browser Combination
Chrome + JAWS
Issue Title
No main landmark defined on the page
Page name
Global
Recommendation for Fix
Use <main> </main> tag to define the main region for the page.
Sr. #
1
Steps to Reproduce
1. Enable JAWS and launch chrome Browser. 2. Load the page https://pristineai.com/ 3. User will be redirected to the 'Home' page. 4. Press 'R' key to navigate to all the regions present on the page. 5. Observe that "Main" region is not present.
Type of Change
Code
WCAG Sc
1.3.1
actual result
The main region is not defined programmatically on the page. As a result, screen reader users might not be able to identify the primary content of the page affectively.
comment
element name
Region
expected result
Screen reader users should be able to identify each landmark region as well as navigate to different regions on the page.

LLM matching rationale
Both findings identify the missing main landmark for the primary page content.
VA-DAT Matched Findings

LLM Finding

LLM (landmark structure check): No `<main>` landmark appears in the payload. Screen reader users rely on it to skip directly to the primary content, so its absence weakens page navigation. The payload has no `_context` heading outline, so it is unclear whether the page content is wrapped in an unlabeled element or is missing a wrapper entirely.

Recommendation for Fix
Use appropriate list mark-up, such as <ul> and <li> to identify related information. In addition, consider applying styles to maintain the visual presentation of a web page.
Sr. #
4
Steps to Reproduce

Type of Change
Code
WCAG Sc
1.3.1
actual result

For example, Links such as "About", "Knowledge", "Our team" and "Everyday Pricing", "Personalized Offers", "Supplier Collaboration" and so on is not marked as unordered list which visually looks like a list of related items. As a result, screen reader users will find it difficult to navigate to the list and different list items quickly.
comment
element name
List
expected result
Related group of items should be identified as list by screen readers. This will help users to understand how many items are present and quickly navigate to different items of the list.

Human finding ID
human-38d4ef5f9de6f67a
Source
Defect report, row 7; scope: global; element: Image
Browser Combination
Chrome + JAWS
Issue Title
Empty alt attribute provided for Logo image .
Page name
Global
Recommendation for Fix
Provide descriptive alternate text such as "Pristine logo" for the image using the "alt" attribute. Ensure that the alternate text describes the purpose of the image.
Sr. #
6
Steps to Reproduce
1. Enable JAWS and launch chrome Browser. 2. Load the page https://pristineai.com/ 3. User will be redirected to the 'Home' page. 4. Navigate to the logo image present in the footer region. 5. Inspect the code and observe that empty alt text is specified for the image.
Type of Change
Code
WCAG Sc
1.1.1
actual result
Empty "alt" attribute is provided for "Pristine logo" image. In the absence of the alternate text, screen reader announces the image as "Unlabelled". As a result, the purpose of the image will not be clear for screen reader users.
comment
element name
Image
expected result
Alternate text of the image link should be read out by the screen reader.

Issue Title
Missing Skip to content link
Page name
Global
Recommendation for Fix
Even though headings are defined on the page and this is sufficient to meet this success criteria, however, in order to assist sighted keyboard-only users, it is recommended that a “Skip to main content” link be provided. This link can be always visible, or it can be positioned off-screen and be displayed when it receives keyboard focus.
Sr. #
7
Steps to Reproduce
1. Enable JAWS and launch chrome Browser. 2. Load the page https://pristineai.com/ 3. User will be redirected to the 'Home' page. 4. Using keyboard navigate on the entire page with tab key. 5. Observe that skip to main content is not available.
Type of Change
Code
WCAG Sc
2.4.1
actual result
"Skip to main content" link is missing to bypass the repeated blocks of content. Due to this, navigating the page becomes more time consuming for sighted keyboard-only users.
comment
element name
Link
expected result
User should be provided with a mechanism to bypass the repeated block of content.
